A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-030-20260915T124111925126Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Direct analyses are to be carried out using the load patch defined in section 4.2 (p, h and la). The pressure to be used is 1.8p where p is determined according to 4.2.2. The load patch must be applied at locations where the capacity of the structure under the combined effects of bending and shear is minimised. In particular, the structure must be checked with a load centred at the UIWL, 0.5h0 below the LIWL, and positioned at several vertical locations in between. Several horizontal locations shall also be checked, especially the locations centred at the mid-span or -spacing. Furthermore, if the load length la cannot be determined directly from the arrangement of the structure, several values of la shall be checked using corresponding values for ca.
Normalized requirement:
Direct analyses are to be carried out using the load patch defined in section 4.2 (p, h and la). The pressure to be used is 1.8p where p is determined according to 4.2.2. The load patch must be applied at locations where the capacity of the structure under the combined effects of bending and shear is minimised. In particular, the structure must be checked with a load centred at the UIWL, 0.5h0 below the LIWL, and positioned at several vertical locations in between. Several horizontal locations shall also be checked, especially the locations centred at the mid-span or -spacing. Furthermore, if the load length la cannot be determined directly from the arrangement of the structure, several values of la shall be checked using corresponding values for ca.
Required inputs: UIWL, LIWL, spacing, span

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix qudtv: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:QuantityValueShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue qudtv:MegaPA ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gen:PressureQuantityValueShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue qudtv:MegaPA ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gen:LengthQuantityValueShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue qudtv:M ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gen:DimensionlessQuantityValueShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue qudtv:UNITLESS ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gen:DirectAnalysisCaseReadinessShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:directAnalysisCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:capacityMinimizingLoadPositionConfirmed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each direct analysis case shall confirm the capacity-minimizing load position."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combinedBendingAndShearEvaluated ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each direct analysis case shall confirm evaluation of combined bending and shear."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:loadPatchLength ;
        sh:node gen:LengthQuantityValueShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each direct analysis case shall provide its load patch length."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:verticalLoadPosition ;
        sh:node gen:LengthQuantityValueShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:horizontalLoadPosition ;
        sh:node gen:LengthQuantityValueShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:verticalLoadPositionType ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:upperIceWaterlineCenteredPosition
            nltl:halfLoadHeightBelowLowerIceWaterlinePosition
            nltl:intermediateVerticalPosition
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:horizontalLoadPositionType ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:midSpanOrSpacingCenteredPosition
            nltl:otherHorizontalPosition
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-030-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:icePressure ;
        sh:node gen:PressureQuantityValueShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall provide the ice pressure input in MPa."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:loadPatchHeight ;
        sh:node gen:LengthQuantityValueShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall provide the load patch height."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:appliedIcePressure ;
        sh:node gen:PressureQuantityValueShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall provide the externally calculated applied ice pressure in MPa."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:loadLengthDeterminedFromArrangement ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall state whether load length was determined from the arrangement."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasDirectAnalysisCase ;
        sh:class nltl:directAnalysisCase ;
        sh:node gen:DirectAnalysisCaseReadinessShape ;
        sh:minCount 2 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall provide several paired direct analysis cases."
    ] ;
    sh:xone (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:loadLengthDeterminedFromArrangement ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:loadLengthDeterminedFromArrangement ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasDirectAnalysisCase ;
                sh:minCount 2 ;
                sh:node gen:DirectAnalysisCaseReadinessShape ;
                sh:and (
                    [
                        sh:property [
                            sh:path nltl:iceLoadAreaFactorCa ;
                            sh:node gen:DimensionlessQuantityValueShape ;
                            sh:minCount 1 ;
                            sh:maxCount 1 ;
                            sh:message "Each direct analysis case shall provide the corresponding load area factor ca."
                        ]
                    ]
                ) ;
                sh:message "When load length is not determined from the arrangement, several cases with corresponding load lengths and ca values are required."
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The direct analysis cases shall include the prescribed vertical positions and a mid-span or spacing-centred horizontal position." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasDirectAnalysisCase ?upperCase .
                        ?upperCase nltl:verticalLoadPositionType
                            nltl:upperIceWaterlineCenteredPosition .
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasDirectAnalysisCase ?halfCase .
                        ?halfCase nltl:verticalLoadPositionType
                            nltl:halfLoadHeightBelowLowerIceWaterlinePosition .
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasDirectAnalysisCase ?intermediateCase .
                        ?intermediateCase nltl:verticalLoadPositionType
                            nltl:intermediateVerticalPosition .
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasDirectAnalysisCase ?midSpanCase .
                        ?midSpanCase nltl:horizontalLoadPositionType
                            nltl:midSpanOrSpacingCenteredPosition .
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF030ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:icePressure ;
        sh:node gen:PressureQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide the ice pressure input in MPa."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:loadPatchHeight ;
        sh:node gen:LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide the load patch height in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:appliedIcePressure ;
        sh:node gen:PressureQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide the externally determined applied ice pressure in MPa."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:loadLengthDeterminedFromArrangement ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasDirectAnalysisCase ;
                sh:class nltl:directAnalysisCase ;
                sh:node gen:DirectAnalysisCaseShape ;
                sh:minCount 2 ;
                sh:message "At least several direct analysis cases are required, including vertical and horizontal load positions."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:loadLengthDeterminedFromArrangement ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasDirectAnalysisCase ;
                sh:class nltl:directAnalysisCase ;
                sh:node gen:ArrangementDependentAnalysisCaseShape ;
                sh:minCount 2 ;
                sh:message "When load length is not determined from the arrangement, several paired load-length and ca analysis cases are required."
            ]
        ]
    ) .

gen:DirectAnalysisCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:directAnalysisCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:verticalLoadPosition ;
        sh:node gen:LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:verticalLoadPositionType ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:or (
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:upperIceWaterlineCenteredPosition ]
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:halfLoadHeightBelowLowerIceWaterlinePosition ]
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:intermediateVerticalPosition ]
        )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:horizontalLoadPosition ;
        sh:node gen:LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:horizontalLoadPositionType ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:or (
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:midSpanOrSpacingCenteredPosition ]
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:otherHorizontalPosition ]
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:upperIceWaterlineCenteredPosition ]
        )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:capacityMinimizingLoadPositionConfirmed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combinedBendingAndShearEvaluated ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] .

gen:ArrangementDependentAnalysisCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:loadPatchLength ;
        sh:node gen:LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadAreaFactorCa ;
        sh:node gen:DimensionlessQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] .

gen:PressureQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;

    ] .
```

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 32
Batch: BATCH_04
Requirement ID: TRF-049
Source family: TRAFICOM
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: Ice Class Regulations and the Application Thereof
Source revision: TRAFICOM/68863/03.04.01.00/2021
Clause / section / page: 4.4.4.1 / The attachment of frames to supporting structures / 21
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-049-20260915T130844234950Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Within the ice-strengthened area, all frames shall be effectively attached to all of the supporting structures. A longitudinal frame shall be attached by brackets to all supporting web frames and bulkheads. When a transversal frame terminates at a stringer or deck, a bracket or similar construction must be fitted. When a frame is running through the supporting structure, both sides of the web plate of the frame must be connected to the structure (by direct welding, collar plate or lug). When a bracket is installed, it must have at least the same thickness as the web plate of the frame and the edge must be appropriately stiffened against buckling.
Normalized requirement:
Within the ice-strengthened area, all frames shall be effectively attached to all of the supporting structures. A longitudinal frame shall be attached by brackets to all supporting web frames and bulkheads. When a transversal frame terminates at a stringer or deck, a bracket or similar construction must be fitted. When a frame is running through the supporting structure, both sides of the web plate of the frame must be connected to the structure (by direct welding, collar plate or lug). When a bracket is installed, it must have at least the same thickness as the web plate of the frame and the edge must be appropriately stiffened against buckling.

```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gs:TRF049_FrameShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:frame ;
    sh:or (
        _:TRF049_WithinIceFalse
        _:TRF049_WithinIceTrue
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Every linked supporting bulkhead or web frame must be effectively attached through a frame attachment." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT ?this
WHERE {
  ?this nltl:withinIceStrengthenedArea true .
  ?this (nltl:hasSupportingBulkhead|nltl:hasSupportingWebFrame) ?support .
  FILTER NOT EXISTS {
    ?this nltl:hasFrameAttachment ?attachment .
    ?attachment nltl:hasAttachedSupportingStructure ?support ;
                nltl:effectiveAttachmentConfirmed true .
  }
}
"""
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A longitudinal frame must be attached by brackets to each linked supporting structure." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

SELECT ?this
WHERE {
  ?this nltl:withinIceStrengthenedArea true ;
        a nltl:longitudinalFrame ;
        (nltl:hasSupportingBulkhead|nltl:hasSupportingWebFrame) ?support .
  FILTER NOT EXISTS {
    ?this nltl:hasFrameAttachment ?attachment .
    ?attachment nltl:hasAttachedSupportingStructure ?support ;
                nltl:hasConnectionBracket ?bracket .
  }
}
"""
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A transverse frame terminating at a deck or ice stringer must have a bracket or similar attachment construction." ;

PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

  ?this nltl:withinIceStrengthenedArea true ;
        a nltl:transverseFrame ;

  FILTER NOT EXISTS {
    ?this nltl:hasFrameAttachment ?attachment .

      EXISTS {

PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT ?this
WHERE {
  ?this nltl:withinIceStrengthenedArea true ;
        nltl:passesThroughSupportingStructure true .
  ?this (nltl:hasSupportingBulkhead|nltl:hasSupportingWebFrame) ?support .

  FILTER (
    NOT EXISTS {
      ?this nltl:webPlateConnectionSideCount ?count .
      FILTER (
        DATATYPE(?count) = xsd:integer &&
        ?count = 2
      )
    }
    ||
    NOT EXISTS {
      ?this nltl:hasFrameAttachment ?attachment .
      ?attachment nltl:hasAttachedSupportingStructure ?support .
      ?support nltl:hasSupportingStructureAttachment ?attachment .
    }
  )
}
"""
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each installed bracket must be at least as thick as the frame web, with a stiffened edge against buckling." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT ?this
WHERE {
  ?this nltl:withinIceStrengthenedArea true ;
        nltl:hasFrameAttachment ?attachment .
  ?attachment nltl:hasConnectionBracket ?bracket .

  FILTER NOT EXISTS {
    ?this nltl:frameWebThickness ?frameThickness .
    ?frameThickness qudt:numericValue ?frameValue ;
                    qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> .
    FILTER (DATATYPE(?frameValue) = xsd:decimal)
  }

  FILTER EXISTS {
    ?this nltl:frameWebThickness ?frameThickness .
    ?frameThickness qudt:numericValue ?frameValue ;
                    qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> .
    ?bracket nltl:bracketThickness ?bracketThickness ;
             nltl:bracketEdgeStiffened true ;
             nltl:bucklingStiffening true .
    ?bracketThickness qudt:numericValue ?bracketValue ;
                      qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> .
    FILTER (
      DATATYPE(?frameValue) = xsd:decimal &&
      DATATYPE(?bracketValue) = xsd:decimal &&
      ?bracketValue < ?frameValue
    )
  }
}
"""
    ] .
```

```turtle
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

                }

    ] .
```

Source revision: TRAFICOM/68863/03.04.01.00/2021
Clause / section / page: 6.1 / Scope / 26
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-069-20260915T132845812058Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
These regulations apply to propulsion machinery covering open- and ducted-type propellers with a controllable pitch or fixed pitch design for ice classes IA Super, IA, IB and IC. The given propeller loads are the expected ice loads for the entire ship's service life under normal operational conditions, including loads resulting from the changing rotational direction of FP propellers. However, these loads do not cover off-design operational conditions, for example when a stopped propeller is dragged through ice. However, the load models of the regulations do not include propeller/ice interaction loads when ice enters the propeller of a turned azimuthing thruster from the side (radially).
Normalized requirement:
The propulsion-machinery rule applies when iceClass IN {'IA Super','IA','IB','IC'} and propeller_type IN {'open','ducted'} and pitch_type IN {'controllable','fixed'}. The design-load scope shall cover normal service-life propeller/ice loads, including FP reversal loads, and shall record that stopped-propeller dragging and radial ice entry into a turned azimuthing thruster are outside the prescribed load-model scope.
Required inputs: iceClass, Qmax_kNm

```turtle
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;

        sh:in (
```

```turtle
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall have one applicable ice class: IA Super, IA, IB, or IC."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in ( "open" "ducted" ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall record one propeller type: open or ducted."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:pitchType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in ( "controllable" "fixed" ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall record one pitch type: controllable or fixed."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumIceTorque ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN-M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The maximum ice torque shall be supplied as a single quantity in kN*m."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For an applicable propulsion-machinery configuration, a linked design condition shall record normal service-life propeller ice loads, inclusion of fixed-pitch propeller reversal loads, and exclusion of stopped-propeller dragging and radial ice-entry loads from the prescribed load model." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                      nltl:propellerType ?propellerType ;
                      nltl:pitchType ?pitchType .

                FILTER (
                    ?iceClass IN (
                        nltl:iceClassIaSuper,
                        nltl:iceClassIa,
                        nltl:iceClassIb,
                        nltl:iceClassIc
                    )
                    && ?propellerType IN ("open", "ducted")
                    && ?pitchType IN ("controllable", "fixed")
                )

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:hasDesignCondition ?condition .
                    ?condition nltl:normalServiceLifePropellerIceLoadCondition true ;
                               nltl:fixedPitchPropellerReversalLoadIncluded true ;
                               nltl:stoppedPropellerDraggingOutsideLoadModel true ;
                               nltl:radialIceEntryOutsideLoadModel true .
                }
            }
        """
    ]
```

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-102-20260915T112652665785Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
In order to meet the functional requirement of paragraph 12.2 above while operating in polar waters, masters, chief mates and officers in charge of a navigational watch shall be qualified in accordance with chapter V of the STCW Convention and the STCW Code, as amended, as follows: Ice conditions | Tankers | Passenger ships | Other. Ice free | Not applicable. | Not applicable. | Not applicable. Open waters | Basic training for master, chief mate and officers in charge of a navigational watch. | Basic training for master, chief mate and officers in charge of a navigational watch. | Not applicable. Other waters | Advanced training for master and chief mate. Basic training for officers in charge of a navigational watch. | Advanced training for master and chief mate. Basic training for officers in charge of a navigational watch. | Advanced training for master and chief mate. Basic training for officers in charge of a navigational watch.
Normalized requirement:
Required polar training level shall be derived from ice_condition, ship_type, and crew_role according to the clause 12.3.1 matrix, and each applicable crew member shall have a valid matching STCW qualification.
Required inputs: Ice condition, ship type, crew manifest, and STCW credentials

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-102-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceConditionClassification ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceFreeIceCondition
            nltl:openWaterIceCondition
            nltl:otherWatersIceCondition
        ) ;
        sh:message "The ship must have exactly one controlled ice condition classification."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarTrainingShipTypeClassification ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:tankerPolarTrainingShipType
            nltl:passengerShipPolarTrainingShipType
            nltl:otherShipPolarTrainingType
        ) ;
        sh:message "The ship must have exactly one controlled polar training ship type."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCrewMemberInventory ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-102-CrewMemberShape>
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-102-CrewMemberShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCrewTrainingRecord ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-102-TrainingRecordShape>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPolarTrainingRecord ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-102-TrainingRecordShape>
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A represented regulated crew member must have exactly one controlled polar training role." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    FILTER EXISTS {
        $this nltl:polarTrainingCrewRoleClassification ?controlledRole .
        FILTER (
            ?controlledRole = nltl:masterPolarTrainingRole ||
            ?controlledRole = nltl:chiefMatePolarTrainingRole ||
            ?controlledRole = nltl:officerInChargeOfNavigationalWatchPolarTrainingRole
        )
    }

    FILTER (
        EXISTS {
            $this nltl:polarTrainingCrewRoleClassification ?role1 ;
                  nltl:polarTrainingCrewRoleClassification ?role2 .
            FILTER (?role1 != ?role2)
            FILTER (
                (?role1 = nltl:masterPolarTrainingRole ||
                 ?role1 = nltl:chiefMatePolarTrainingRole ||
                 ?role1 = nltl:officerInChargeOfNavigationalWatchPolarTrainingRole)
                &&
                (?role2 = nltl:masterPolarTrainingRole ||
                 ?role2 = nltl:chiefMatePolarTrainingRole ||
                 ?role2 = nltl:officerInChargeOfNavigationalWatchPolarTrainingRole)
            )
        }
        ||
        EXISTS {
            $this nltl:polarTrainingCrewRoleClassification ?otherRole .
            FILTER (
                ?otherRole != nltl:masterPolarTrainingRole &&
                ?otherRole != nltl:chiefMatePolarTrainingRole &&
                ?otherRole != nltl:officerInChargeOfNavigationalWatchPolarTrainingRole
            )
        }
    )
}
"""
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "An applicable crew member must have a training record with the matrix-required training level and a valid STCW qualification." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    ?ship nltl:hasCrewMemberInventory $this ;
          nltl:iceConditionClassification ?ice ;
          nltl:polarTrainingShipTypeClassification ?shipType .
    $this nltl:polarTrainingCrewRoleClassification ?role .

    FILTER (
        (
            ?ice = nltl:openWaterIceCondition &&
            ?shipType != nltl:otherShipPolarTrainingType &&
            (
                ?role = nltl:masterPolarTrainingRole ||
                ?role = nltl:chiefMatePolarTrainingRole ||
                ?role = nltl:officerInChargeOfNavigationalWatchPolarTrainingRole
            )
        )
        ||
        (
            ?ice = nltl:otherWatersIceCondition &&
            (
                ?role = nltl:masterPolarTrainingRole ||
                ?role = nltl:chiefMatePolarTrainingRole ||
                ?role = nltl:officerInChargeOfNavigationalWatchPolarTrainingRole
            )
        )
    )

    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        {
            ?this nltl:hasCrewTrainingRecord ?record .
        }
        UNION
        {
            ?this nltl:hasPolarTrainingRecord ?record .
        }

        ?record nltl:requiredTrainingLevel ?requiredLevel ;
                nltl:trainingRecordLevel ?recordLevel ;
                nltl:stcwQualificationValid true .

        FILTER (
            (
                ?ice = nltl:openWaterIceCondition &&
                ?shipType != nltl:otherShipPolarTrainingType &&
                ?requiredLevel = nltl:basicPolarTrainingLevel
            )
            ||
            (
                ?ice = nltl:otherWatersIceCondition &&
                (
                    (
                        (
                            ?role = nltl:masterPolarTrainingRole ||
                            ?role = nltl:chiefMatePolarTrainingRole
                        ) &&
                        ?requiredLevel = nltl:advancedPolarTrainingLevel
                    )
                    ||
                    (
                        ?role = nltl:officerInChargeOfNavigationalWatchPolarTrainingRole &&
                        ?requiredLevel = nltl:basicPolarTrainingLevel
                    )
                )
            )
        )

        FILTER (?recordLevel = ?requiredLevel)
    }
}
"""
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-102-TrainingRecordShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredTrainingLevel ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:basicPolarTrainingLevel
            nltl:advancedPolarTrainingLevel
            nltl:polarTrainingNotApplicable
        ) ;
        sh:message "A polar training record must state exactly one controlled required training level."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:trainingRecordLevel ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:basicPolarTrainingLevel
            nltl:advancedPolarTrainingLevel
            nltl:polarTrainingNotApplicable
        ) ;
        sh:message "A polar training record must state exactly one controlled recorded training level."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:stcwQualificationValid ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The recorded training level must equal the required training level." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:requiredTrainingLevel ?required ;
          nltl:trainingRecordLevel ?recorded .
    FILTER (?required != ?recorded)
}
"""
    ] .

```

Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-033-20260915T103736185422Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
For ships operating in areas and during periods where ice accretion is likely to occur, the following icing allowance shall be made in the stability calculations: 30 kg/m^2 on exposed weather decks and gangways; 7.5 kg/m^2 for the projected lateral area of each side of the ship above the water plane; and the projected lateral area of discontinuous surfaces of rail, sundry booms, spars (except masts) and rigging of ships having no sails and the projected lateral area of other small objects shall be computed by increasing the total projected area of continuous surfaces by 5% and the static moments of this area by 10%.
Normalized requirement:
IF ice_accretion_likely = true, THEN deck_icing_allowance_kg_m2 = 30, side_icing_allowance_kg_m2 = 7.5, discontinuous_surface_projected_area = continuous_surface_projected_area * 1.05, and discontinuous_surface_static_moment = continuous_surface_static_moment * 1.10.
Required inputs: Ice-accretion applicability and stability-calculation inputs/results

CASE IMO-033-F01
Original expected verdict: FAIL

```turtle
<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-033-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceAccretionLikely ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "iceAccretionLikely must be supplied as a single boolean value."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceAccretionLikely ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:continuousSurfaceProjectedArea ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;

                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [

                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minInclusive "30"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxInclusive "30"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:KiloGM-PER-M2 ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:sideIcingAllowance ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minInclusive "7.5"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxInclusive "7.5"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:KiloGM-PER-M2 ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:discontinuousSurfaceProjectedArea ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:M2 ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:discontinuousSurfaceStaticMoment ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:KiloN-M ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "discontinuousSurfaceProjectedArea must equal continuousSurfaceProjectedArea multiplied by 1.05 within a relative tolerance of 1e-6, with an absolute fallback of 1e-9." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:continuousSurfaceProjectedArea ?continuousNode ;
                              nltl:discontinuousSurfaceProjectedArea ?discontinuousNode .
                        ?continuousNode qudt:numericValue ?continuousValue .
                        ?discontinuousNode qudt:numericValue ?discontinuousValue .
                        FILTER (
                            ABS(?discontinuousValue - (?continuousValue * 1.05))
                            >
                            (ABS(?continuousValue * 1.05) * 0.000001 + 0.000000001)
                        )
                    }
                """
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "discontinuousSurfaceStaticMoment must equal continuousSurfaceStaticMoment multiplied by 1.10 within a relative tolerance of 1e-6, with an absolute fallback of 1e-9." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:continuousSurfaceStaticMoment ?continuousNode ;
                              nltl:discontinuousSurfaceStaticMoment ?discontinuousNode .
                        ?continuousNode qudt:numericValue ?continuousValue .
                        ?discontinuousNode qudt:numericValue ?discontinuousValue .
                        FILTER (
                            ABS(?discontinuousValue - (?continuousValue * 1.10))
                            >
                            (ABS(?continuousValue * 1.10) * 0.000001 + 0.000000001)
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceAccretionLikely ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF005Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An ice class is required as an input for this requirement."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "A construction contract date is required to determine applicability of the 2002 Ice Class Regulations."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "A construction stage date is required to determine applicability of the 2002 Ice Class Regulations."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2002 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Ships meeting the stated dates shall identify the 2002 Ice Class Regulations as the applicable edition."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "The 2002 Ice Class Regulations applicability branch requires a construction stage on or after 1 September 2003 and a contract before 1 January 2010." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:constructionContractDate ?contract ;
                              nltl:constructionStageDate ?stage .
                        FILTER (
                            datatype(?contract) = xsd:date &&
                            datatype(?stage) = xsd:date
                        )
                        FILTER (
                            !(
                                (
                                    YEAR(?stage) > 2003 ||
                                    (
                                        YEAR(?stage) = 2003 &&
                                        (
                                            MONTH(?stage) > 9 ||
                                            (MONTH(?stage) = 9 && DAY(?stage) >= 1)
                                        )
                                    )
                                ) &&
                                (
                                    YEAR(?contract) < 2010 ||
                                    (
                                        YEAR(?contract) = 2010 &&
                                        MONTH(?contract) = 1 &&
                                        DAY(?contract) < 1
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "A construction contract date is required to determine applicability of the 2002 Ice Class Regulations."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "A construction stage date is required to determine applicability of the 2002 Ice Class Regulations."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "The 2002 Ice Class Regulations shall not be selected when the stated construction dates do not meet their applicability condition." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:constructionContractDate ?contract ;
                              nltl:constructionStageDate ?stage .
                        FILTER (
                            datatype(?contract) = xsd:date &&
                            datatype(?stage) = xsd:date
                        )
                        FILTER (
                            !(
                                (
                                    YEAR(?stage) > 2003 ||
                                    (
                                        YEAR(?stage) = 2003 &&
                                        (
                                            MONTH(?stage) > 9 ||
                                            (MONTH(?stage) = 9 && DAY(?stage) >= 1)
                                        )
                                    )
                                ) &&
                                (
                                    YEAR(?contract) < 2010 ||
                                    (
                                        YEAR(?contract) = 2010 &&

                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        )
                        FILTER EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition
                                  nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2002 .
                        }
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-005-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction contract date is required to determine applicability of the 2002 Ice Class Regulations."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction stage date is required to determine applicability of the 2002 Ice Class Regulations."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-005-Applicable2002Shape>
        <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-005-NotApplicableShape>
    ) .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-005-Applicable2002Shape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2002 ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Ships constructed under the stated date conditions shall identify the 2002 Ice Class Regulations as the applicable regulation edition."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The construction dates do not establish applicability of the 2002 Ice Class Regulations for this branch." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?stage ;
                      nltl:constructionContractDate ?contract .
                FILTER (datatype(?stage) = xsd:date && datatype(?contract) = xsd:date)
                FILTER (
                    YEAR(?stage) < 2003 ||
                    (YEAR(?stage) = 2003 &&
                        (MONTH(?stage) < 9 ||
                         (MONTH(?stage) = 9 && DAY(?stage) < 1))) ||
                    YEAR(?contract) >= 2010
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-005-NotApplicableShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The construction dates establish applicability of the 2002 Ice Class Regulations, so the applicable regulation edition must be identified." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?stage ;
                      nltl:constructionContractDate ?contract .
                FILTER (datatype(?stage) = xsd:date && datatype(?contract) = xsd:date)
                FILTER (
                    YEAR(?stage) > 2003 ||
                    (YEAR(?stage) = 2003 &&
                        (MONTH(?stage) > 9 ||
                         (MONTH(?stage) = 9 && DAY(?stage) >= 1))) &&
                    YEAR(?contract) < 2010
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-005> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction contract date is required to determine applicability of the 2002 Ice Class Regulations."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction stage date is required to determine applicability of the 2002 Ice Class Regulations."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a ship whose construction stage began on or after 1 September 2003 and whose construction contract predates 1 January 2010, the applicable regulation edition must be the 2002 Ice Class Regulations."
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?stageDate ;
                      nltl:constructionContractDate ?contractDate .

                FILTER (
                    (
                        YEAR(?stageDate) > 2003 ||
                        (
                            YEAR(?stageDate) = 2003 &&
                            (
                                MONTH(?stageDate) > 9 ||
                                (MONTH(?stageDate) = 9 && DAY(?stageDate) >= 1)
                            )
                        )
                    ) &&
                    YEAR(?contractDate) < 2010
                )

                {
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition
                              nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2002 .
                    }
                }
                UNION
                {
                    $this nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition ?edition .
                    FILTER (
                        ?edition != nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2002
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMember ;
        sh:node gen:StructuralMemberShape ;
        sh:class nltl:structuralMember
    ] .

gen:StructuralMemberShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:not [
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:hasSpannedHullArea ;
                    sh:minCount 1
                ]
            ]
        ]
        gen:SpanningStructuralMemberShape
    ) .

gen:SpanningStructuralMemberShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasSpannedHullArea ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:HullAreaValueShape ;
        sh:class nltl:hullAreaValue ;
        sh:message "Each structural member spanning across a hull-area boundary must identify at least one spanned hull area."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The selected hull area factor must be a single dimensionless quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "selectedHullAreaFactor must equal the largest spanned hull area factor." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ?selectedNode .
                ?selectedNode qudt:numericValue ?observed .

                {
                    SELECT ?member (MAX(?factor) AS ?expected)
                    WHERE {
                        ?member nltl:hasSpannedHullArea ?area .
                        ?area nltl:hullAreaFactor ?factorNode .
                        ?factorNode qudt:numericValue ?factor .
                        FILTER (?member = $this)
                    }
                    GROUP BY ?member
                }

                FILTER (?observed != ?expected)
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:HullAreaValueShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hullAreaFactor ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Each spanned hull area must provide a single dimensionless hull area factor."
    ] .

```

```turtle
gs:ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMember ;
        sh:node gs:StructuralMemberShape
    ] .

gs:StructuralMemberShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:structuralMember ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasSpannedHullArea ;
        sh:node gs:HullAreaValueShape
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "A structural member spanning one or more hull areas must have a selected hull area factor." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasSpannedHullArea ?area .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ?selectedNode .
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The selected hull area factor must equal the largest hull area factor among the hull areas spanned by the structural member." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ?selectedNode .
                ?selectedNode qudt:numericValue ?selectedValue .
                $this nltl:hasSpannedHullArea ?area .
                ?area nltl:hullAreaFactor ?factorNode .
                ?factorNode qudt:numericValue ?factorValue .
            }
            GROUP BY $this ?selectedValue
            HAVING (?selectedValue != MAX(?factorValue))
        """
    ] .

gs:HullAreaValueShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:hullAreaValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hullAreaFactor ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-019> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMember ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:structuralMember ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasSpannedHullArea ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class nltl:hullAreaValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:hullAreaFactor ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Each structural member spanning one or more hull areas must use the largest spanned hull area factor." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT DISTINCT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?member nltl:hasSpannedHullArea ?area .

                {
                    SELECT ?member (MAX(?factorNumber) AS ?maximumFactor)
                    WHERE {
                        ?member nltl:hasSpannedHullArea ?areaForMaximum .
                        ?areaForMaximum nltl:hullAreaFactor ?factorNode .
                        ?factorNode a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            qudt:numericValue ?factorNumber ;
                            qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .
                        FILTER (datatype(?factorNumber) = xsd:decimal)
                    }
                    GROUP BY ?member
                }

                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?member nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ?selectedNode
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        ?member nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ?invalidSelectedNode .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?invalidSelectedNode a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                                qudt:numericValue ?invalidNumber ;
                                qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .
                            FILTER (datatype(?invalidNumber) = xsd:decimal)
                        }
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?member nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ?selectedNodeForComparison .
                        ?selectedNodeForComparison a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            qudt:numericValue ?selectedNumber ;
                            qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .
                        FILTER (datatype(?selectedNumber) = xsd:decimal)
                        FILTER (
                            ABS(?selectedNumber - ?maximumFactor)
                            <= IF(
                                ABS(?maximumFactor) * 0.000001 > 0.000000001,
                                ABS(?maximumFactor) * 0.000001,
                                0.000000001
```
